# Synthetic Multi-Variation Voice Dataset Summary (37 Calls)

- **Generated Calls**: **37**
- **Total Duration**: **5261.3 s (87.69 mins)**
- **Configured Conversation Duration Range**: **`120.0s – 180.0s`** (Target Avg: **`145.0s`**)
- **Actual Conversation Duration Range**: **`120.2s – 179.7s`** (Actual Avg: **`142.2s`**)
- **TTS Engine**: **gemini-3.8-flash-tts** (Google GenAI SDK `google-genai`)
- **Scenario Generator LLM**: **gemini-3.8-flash**
- **Sample Rates Exported**: `16 kHz` wideband (`audio_16k/*.wav`) & `8 kHz` telephony (`audio_8k/*.wav`)

## Ground-Truth Segment Breakdown

| Segment `label` | `should_trigger` | Count |
| :--- | :---: | ---: |
| `voice` (Primary User Speech — clean, noisy, overlapped, micro-utterances, laughs, hesitations, angry/fast/slow, packet-loss) | **`true` (`1`)** | 641 |
| `noise` (Compound Multi-Noise & Wind Buffeting Bursts in silence) | **`false` (`0`)** | 397 |
| `background_voice` (Far-Field, Near-Field Loud, TV/Radio, 2-Speaker Babble, Bot Echo, Bystander Backchannels & Coughs) | **`false` (`0`)** | 215 |
| **Total Segments** | — | **1253** |
